Every C controller and neural multiplier stayed exactly initial; frozen C chose rock on all 69 observed rounds, scored **24/72**, and failed reliability. This matched negative control supports reward-dependent B learning under this design. Neither B nor C received correct-action labels, future moves, hidden strategy, or a direct previous-move lookup; their three inputs were the actual simulated, standardized MBON rates.
- **Locked decision / limitation / stop.** The full success criterion **failed** because B **did not strictly outperform A on seed 712**: both won 65/72. B outperformed A on the other three seeds, but the predeclared every-seed paired gate cannot be relaxed to a tie or mean advantage. This is encouraging **four-of-four student-level three-action learning on one deterministic opponent**, not a successful locked all-gates comparison, universal reliability, dopamine-only learning, or neural teaching. It does not isolate whether action-specific credit, epsilon exploration, or their combination caused the state-0 gain. No epsilon/learning-rate retuning, new architecture, additional seeds, follow-up diagnostic, or teacher-student experiment follows this failed full gate. Preserve raw per-trial neural rates/features/action values/actions/rewards, training curves, parameters, and validity/gate flags in `data/rps_contextual_bandit.json`. Reproduce once in a copy without the output using `node tools/check_rps_contextual_bandit.mjs`; the runner refuses overwrite.

### Prospective RPS teacher-source information study (locked before held-out analysis, )

- **Hypothesis and information bound.** The `previous_opponent` opponent has offset 1 and zero noise: after the first round of each episode, its next move is `(previousOpponent + 1) % 3`. Fly B receives that previous move through its VPN cue until the 240 ms decision. Thus next-move conditional information beyond Fly B's cue is exactly zero on observed rounds. A teacher signal could at most recode the cue or a learned response in a form that speeds learning. The contextual-bandit teacher stores learning in an external action-value readout while KC-to-MBON synaptic writes are frozen, so predecision MBON activity may remain sensory rather than training-dependent.
- **Single fixed source and encoding.** Examine only the three existing action-group MBON mean spike counts at the 240 ms decision, counted since the 50 ms reset. They are actual simulated neural activity and the established inputs to the contextual controller; no action value, probability, selected action, reward, or hidden-rule value enters the signal. Preserve the raw three-number vector; apply only the previously used `log1p` transform for an external nearest-Euclidean-centroid information analysis. Reuse centroids calibrated exclusively on reward-free seeds **651-653** in `data/rps_readout_information.json`; do not refit them. Held-out trained teacher seeds are **712-714**, selected consecutively before this analysis and independent of calibration; their archived B and untrained C 72-round records supply 69 observed-cue rounds per seed. Seed 711 is not used in the held-out source gate. The first cue-free round of each episode is excluded from cue-conditional metrics. No receiver is constructed.
- **Predeclared measurements, controls and gates.** Apply the fixed centroid to trained and untrained raw MBON vectors to classify previous move, and thereby the next move under the already fixed offset-1 opponent; report total, by-state and by-teacher accuracy. Compare with 1000 within-teacher shuffled previous-move labels (fixed RNG seed 20261104), 1/3 balanced chance, and the previous-move lookup (207/207 deterministic next moves). The **information gate** requires trained accuracy at least **80% overall**, at least **70% for each of the three observed states**, and greater than the shuffled 95th-percentile score. The **training-dependence gate** requires at least **6 of 12** matched reward-free probes **for every state and each held-out teacher** to differ from its untrained counterpart by more than `1e-9` in at least one raw MBON rate. These 12 probes/state use identical cue, frozen all-one neural weights, state reset, and seeded neural streams in trained and untrained copies; sample before action/reward. Record whether trained and untrained actions differ afterward, separately from the neural signal. Compare archived B/C trial rates as a secondary history-sensitive check; any difference there alone cannot establish an intrinsic learned neural source because prior actions and rewards diverge. Both gates must pass to justify a receiving experiment; failure stops this source search. Run no teacher-student training.
- **Planned artifact and command.** `node tools/check_rps_teacher_source_information.mjs` will read saved recordings and perform only the small frozen matched probes; it refuses overwrite of `data/rps_teacher_source_information.json`. Preserve prior locked failures and all original cue results.

### RPS teacher-source information result: sensory information passes, training dependence fails

- **Experiment and controls.** Used exactly the predeclared three action-group MBON rates, fixed calibration centroids, archived contextual-bandit teachers **712-714**, their matched untrained controls, and 1000 within-teacher label shuffles. The archived trained and untrained evaluation records each contain **207** observed-state rounds (state counts **69/72/66**); each next opponent move equals `(previous + 1) % 3`. We also ran only the declared **108** reward-free matched reset probes (three teachers, three states, 12 repeats), restoring saved trained and untrained controller parameters with all-one KC-to-MBON multipliers. The two copies had identical sensory inputs, neural resets, and neural random streams. Probe source rates were sampled before action or reward; neither neural nor controller weights changed.
- **Information results.** The fixed external centroid classified the trained teacher's previous state on **181/207 (87.4%)** rounds, equivalent to **181/207 next-move classifications** under this deterministic opponent. Per-state scores were **58/69 (84.1%)**, **58/72 (80.6%)**, and **65/66 (98.5%)**; per-teacher scores were **61/69, 58/69, 62/69**. Untrained controls scored **188/207 (90.8%)**, with per-state scores **58/69, 65/72, 65/66** and per-teacher scores **63/69, 67/69, 58/69**. Balanced chance is **69/207**; the fixed shuffled-label mean was **68.954/207** and its 95th-percentile score **80/207**. The trained source passes the locked information gate. Fly B's direct previous-move lookup predicts **207/207** observed next moves, so the teacher provides **zero conditional next-move information** beyond B's cue by task construction. The external classifier is analysis only, never installed in a fly.
- **Training-dependence result and interpretation.** In matched reward-free probes, trained and untrained MBON rate vectors differed on **0/12 for every state of every teacher**, far below the locked **6/12** gate. Yet their controller actions differed on **97/108** probes, confirming that the saved readouts differed while the sampled neural source did not. In full archived evaluation episodes, trained and untrained MBON vectors differed on **66/69, 66/69, 65/69** matched rounds; those histories contain different actions and rewards, so this does not identify an intrinsic training-dependent source. The trained contextual readout stores its learning outside the frozen KC-to-MBON synapses and does not feed its values back into the predecision neural signal. Thus this candidate is informative about the sensory cue but indistinguishable from untrained neural activity when cue and neural history are controlled. **The combined source gate fails.** No receiving or teacher-student learning-speed experiment is justified with this source; no alternative source search or interface was started. This is a separate exploratory negative result and leaves the contextual-bandit experiment's failed strict paired gate unchanged.
- **Raw result / reproduction.** `data/rps_teacher_source_information.json` contains the locked design, centroids, trial-level trained/untrained rates and external predictions, per-seed summaries, shuffle scores, all matched probes, and gate flags. Run `node tools/check_rps_teacher_source_information.mjs` in a copy without that output file; the script refuses overwrite. `node --check tools/check_rps_teacher_source_information.mjs` passed. Earlier data, cue results, and model code were not changed.

### Prospective hybrid RPS teacher-message source test (locked before calibration and held-out scoring, )

- **Question and scope.** A contextual-bandit Fly A learned action values from real MBON features, while its KC-to-MBON synapses were frozen. Test one restricted, training-dependent *confidence* message without sending the controller's action or weights. This is a hybrid artificial-controller hypothesis, distinct from original dopamine-only teaching. The old contextual-bandit all-gates comparison remains failed because seed 712 tied its nonlinear baseline. No Fly B receiver or learning run is part of this source test.
- **One candidate and encoding.** From the existing three standardized action-group MBON rates `f`, compute the saved trained teacher's three action-value scores `Q_a(f)`. Let `d=max_a Q_a(f)-min_a Q_a(f)`, an action-name-permutation-invariant spread. Send only `m=1{d>tau}` (one bit); no scores, weights, chosen action, target, or opponent rule leave Fly A. Set `tau` to the median of `d` over the **69 observed-cue archived frozen rounds of teacher 711**, using no correctness labels or held-out teachers. Strict `>` fixes ties. The untrained counterpart has three zero score heads. A high spread could indicate learned confidence, but it may correlate with a particular winning action; the leakage gates below must reject that case. This is the only candidate and threshold rule; no alternate contrast or cutoff will be tried.
- **Calibration and independent held-out data.** Teacher **711** is calibration only. Teachers **712-714** are independent held-out seeds. For each, use the **36 previously saved, reward-free, neural-reset probes** (12 each for previous opponent moves 0/1/2) from `data/rps_teacher_source_information.json`, and the saved B/C controller parameters from `data/rps_contextual_bandit.json`. The paired trained/untrained probes have identical actual simulator MBON rates, cue, neural weights, and streams. Recompute standardized features and scores without calling any learning method; verify trained argmax matches the archived action. Exclude cue-free rounds. The previous move is already observed by Fly B, and the zero-noise opponent's next move is `(previous+1)%3`; conditional next-move information beyond B's cue is **exactly zero** regardless of message statistics.
- **Predeclared calibration screen and held-out gates.** First check seed 711 before reading held-out score spreads. The candidate is rejected as an encoded answer if either bit occurs on fewer than **20%** of its 69 rounds, or if, within either bit bin, more than **75%** of rounds share the same teacher-selected action *or* the same winning action. If calibration passes, freeze `tau` and score all three held-out teachers without revision. Held-out **training-dependence** requires trained bit 1 on at least **20%** of each teacher's 36 probes and untrained bit 1 on at most **5%**; **non-saturation** requires each trained bit frequency between **20% and 80%** per teacher. **No encoded answer** requires no bit bin of any held-out teacher to have more than **75%** of one selected or winning action. **State information** requires pooled empirical mutual information `I(message; previous move)` at least **0.10 bit**, greater than the 95th percentile of **1000** within-teacher message shuffles using RNG seed **20261104**, and at least **0.03 bit** separately for each teacher. Report full state-by-bit and selected/correct-action-by-bit counts, trained/untrained/shuffled controls, bit frequencies, and the perfect previous-move lookup. All gates must pass to justify future receiving investigation; stop at the first failed stage. A passing source gate still would not demonstrate teaching.
- **Budget and reproduction.** Analysis of saved recordings only; no new simulation, training, channel implementation, or parameter search. Planned command `node tools/check_rps_hybrid_message_source.mjs`; write-only-new artifact `data/rps_hybrid_message_source.json` with overwrite refusal. Preserve all previous raw files and gates.

### Hybrid RPS teacher-message source result: action-leak gate fails

- **Calibration and validity.** The one fixed confidence-spread threshold was the median on teacher 711: **`tau = 1.098235868125541`**. Its 69 observed-cue rounds produced **35 zero / 34 one** bits; both bins passed the prospective <=75% selected- and winning-action purity screen. Recomputed controller scores and argmax actions matched the archived records. The held-out check used only the previously saved **36 reward-free neural-reset probes per teacher** for seeds 712-714. Each trained/untrained pair had the same MBON rates, and the reconstructed trained argmax matched the archived action. No simulator, controller, neural weight, or Fly B receiver was changed or trained.
- **Held-out message and information.** Bit-0/bit-1 counts were **16/20** (712), **22/14** (713), and **14/22** (714); all passed the 20-80% non-saturation gate. The matched untrained zero-initialized controllers emitted bit 1 on **0/36** probes for every seed, so the bit passed the predeclared training-dependence gate. Empirical `I(bit; previous move)` was **0.234, 0.688, 0.367 bits** by teacher and **0.312 bits pooled**, above the fixed 1000-shuffle 95th percentile **0.0396 bits** (shuffle mean **0.0136**). The state-information gate passed. Fly B already observes that state, which determines the next opponent move; conditional next-move information beyond B's cue remains **zero**.
- **Decisive failure / control.** The held-out no-encoded-answer gate **failed**. For teacher **712**, bit 0 coincided with the teacher selecting scissors on **13/16 (81.3%)** probes, above the locked 75% maximum. For teacher **713**, bit 1 coincided with the teacher selecting scissors on **12/14 (85.7%)** and with rock being the correct winning action on **12/14 (85.7%)** probes. Teacher 714 passed the leakage screen, but all held-out teachers had to pass. The bit is not a literal three-way action code, and it does not reveal the hidden rule or weights; nevertheless it acts as an overly action-specific indicator in independent teachers under the predeclared criterion. We reject it rather than relabel the observed association as pure confidence. The full source gate **fails**; no receiving or learning-speed test is justified with this message. There was no threshold adjustment, second candidate, stimulation, student training, or teacher-student communication run. This separate exploratory negative result leaves the contextual-bandit strict paired failure and earlier cue/RPS results unchanged.
- **Raw records / reproduction.** `data/rps_hybrid_message_source.json` preserves the threshold, calibration and held-out bit/state/action contingency counts, trained and untrained bits, controller score spreads, fixed shuffle comparison, and every gate. Reproduce in a copy without the artifact using `node tools/check_rps_hybrid_message_source.mjs`; the runner refuses overwrite. `node --check tools/check_rps_hybrid_message_source.mjs` passed.

### Prospective hybrid RPS KC receiving gate (locked before simulation, )

- **Scope and source.** A new exploratory hybrid teaching protocol explicitly permits action-related advice. Keep the previously fixed teacher bit `1{max(Q)-min(Q)>1.098235868125541}` from the learned contextual-bandit response to real MBON rates. The preceding source study **failed its no-action-leak gate** and is not reclassified as a success; this new protocol permits that association. The bit cannot add next-move information beyond Fly B's observed previous move under the deterministic opponent. The question is whether a neural message can alter learning efficiency. No teacher weights, scores, labels, hidden rule, or direct student-controller update will be transmitted.
- **One receiving pathway and necessary timing adaptation.** Reuse the earlier one-bit KC targets: bit 0 drives KC indices **506,545**, bit 1 drives **113,1421**, at the previously used **300 Hz for 40 ms**. All four are KC neurons, distinct from RPS's VPN cue neurons. RPS resets counts at 50 ms and decides at 240 ms; use a single fixed **50-90 ms** receiving window so message-evoked spikes can enter the unchanged 50-240 ms MBON decision count. The original 150 Hz previous-move VPN cue stays on from 0 to 240 ms in every condition, including no message. There is no stimulation of action MBONs or change to neural dynamics/readout. Do not change targets, strength, or timing after outcomes.
- **Frozen diagnostic sample and controls.** Preselect one new untrained Fly B seed **721**, real wiring, all-one KC-to-MBON multipliers, zero contextual-bandit controller, frozen learning. For each of three previous-move cues and **12** fixed neural streams (`rng(721*100000+repeat+1)`), run matched reward-free, state-reset 240 ms probes with **no message**, **bit 0**, and **bit 1**. Reset counts at 50 ms; turn the corresponding KC drive on at 50 and off at 90; remove the unchanged VPN cue at 240, then read the same three action-group MBON rates and standardized controller input features as the existing RPS readout. Record both target-pair spike counts and active KC identities at 90 ms, all KC recruitment by 240 ms, MBON rates/features, and frozen neural/controller weight integrity. No opponent outcome, action reward, dopamine update, teacher-student training, or communication architecture change occurs in this stage.
- **Predeclared validity, physical receiving and interference gates.** Validity requires identical input cue, initial neural weights, and stream seed across the three conditions for each matched probe; actual target neurons must be KCs, the original VPN cue must remain active through the decision, and all weights must remain unchanged. **Physical recruitment:** for each cue and each bit, the driven KC pair's mean 50-90 ms spike count must exceed its matched no-message mean by at least **1 spike**. **Readout distinguishability:** train nearest-Euclidean-centroid bit classifiers on standardized three-MBON features from repeats 0-5 separately for each cue, then score untouched repeats 6-11; require at least **10/12** bit classifications for **every cue**, including at least **5/6** for each bit. **Cue preservation:** apply the already fixed, reward-free `log1p` three-MBON state centroids from `data/rps_readout_information.json` without refitting. Require no-message state classification at least **9/12 for each cue**; for each message value require at least **8/12 for each cue** and no more than a **2/12** drop relative to no message on that cue. Any failed validity, receiving, or interference condition stops before student training. This is one fixed diagnostic, with no strength/timing/source search.
- **Conditional next stage.** Only after all gates pass, prospectively declare fresh independent teacher-student seeds, matched four-arm teaching controls, frozen evaluation both with and without messages, learning-speed and retained-learning gates, and stopping rules before running any training. A passing receiving gate alone would not establish teaching. Planned diagnostic command `node tools/check_rps_hybrid_receiver.mjs`; new artifact `data/rps_hybrid_receiver.json` with overwrite refusal.

### Hybrid RPS KC receiving result: bit decoding and cue preservation fail

- **Execution and validity.** Ran the one predeclared, reward-free frozen diagnostic on seed **721** only: three observed previous-move cues, 12 matched neural streams each, and no-message/bit-0/bit-1 KC conditions (**108 probes**). All targets were KCs, separate from the unchanged 0-240 ms VPN cue; message drive was exactly 300 Hz at 50-90 ms, and all-one KC-to-MBON multipliers and zero contextual-controller parameters remained unchanged. No teacher-student training, reward, dopamine update, or model change occurred. The old one-bit confidence source's action-leak failure remains a valid prior result, though this new protocol allowed action-related advice.
- **Physical response.** Relative to matched no-message probes, the driven KC pair gained a mean **14.83/15.33** spikes for bits 0/1 on cue 0, **15.25/15.67** on cue 1, and **16.08/15.67** on cue 2 during 50-90 ms; no-message targets had zero spikes in that window. Mean active KCs by 240 ms were **7.92/10.33/10.92** (none/0/1) on cue 0, **7.17/7.67/10.92** on cue 1, and **7.67/8.50/10.83** on cue 2. All three action-group MBON rates and active KC identities were recorded for each probe. The physical recruitment gate passed: the stimulation reaches the network and affects the readout features.
- **Distinguishability and interference.** A per-cue bit decoder calibrated on repeats 0-5 and scored on untouched repeats 6-11 classified **12/12, 12/12, 8/12** bits for cues 0/1/2. On cue 2, bit 0 was identified only **2/6** times, failing the locked >=10/12 and >=5/6 requirements. The older fixed MBON state decoder recognized no-message cue 0/1/2 on **10/12, 12/12, 12/12**; with bit 0 it scored **12/12, 12/12, 12/12**, and with bit 1 **9/12, 4/12, 11/12**. Bit 1 therefore caused a severe cue-1 recognition drop of **8/12**, exceeding the locked 2/12 maximum and missing the 8/12 minimum. The cue-preservation gate failed. The bit-1 cue-1 mean MBON rates shifted from **[0.119, 0.018, 0.024]** without a message to **[0.310, 0.274, 0.548]**; this is a measured representation change, not evidence of helpful learning.
- **Decision and limits.** Validity and target recruitment passed, but **both** readout-input bit distinguishability and cue preservation failed, so the receiving gate failed. Stop before any fresh-pair training, correct/shuffled/untrained/disconnected teaching arms, or message-on/off evaluation. We cannot infer immediate decision help, learning acceleration, or retained independent benefit. No target, timing, strength, source bit, threshold, or success gate was adjusted. The fixed opponent still offers zero next-move information beyond Fly B's observed previous move; any eventual benefit would have to be faster learning, which remains untested with a viable receiver.
- **Raw result / reproduction.** `data/rps_hybrid_receiver.json` stores every frozen probe's cue, stream, target spikes, active KC identities, MBON rates, standardized readout features, state prediction, calibration/test bit predictions, summary gates, and stop decision. Reproduce in a copy without that artifact with `node tools/check_rps_hybrid_receiver.mjs`; the runner refuses overwrite. `node --check tools/check_rps_hybrid_receiver.mjs` passed.

### Proposed temporally separated RPS receiver: stopped at timing feasibility

- **Hypothesis / requested single change.** Test whether moving the same fixed 300 Hz, 40 ms one-bit stimulation of KC pairs **506/545** (bit 0) and **113/1421** (bit 1) to a window ending before Fly B's unchanged RPS sensory cue begins would avoid the observed interference. The teacher threshold remains **1.098235868125541**. The preceding 50-90 ms receiver remains a valid failure: cue-2 bit decoding **8/12** versus required **10/12**, and cue-1 recognition **4/12** under bit 1 versus **12/12** disconnected.
- **Read-only timing audit / result.** In `js/rps.js`, each 400 ms round starts Fly B's previous-opponent VPN cue at phase **0** and leaves it on through the phase-**240** decision; counts reset at **50**, KC eligibility and the hybrid MBON input are captured at **240**, reward follows immediately, and the dopamine update occurs at **340**. The old diagnostic's **50-90 ms** KC drive therefore overlapped the cue. A message that finishes before the *same round's* cue begins must end by phase **0**, but the current round has no pre-cue phase. The apparent prior-round **360-400 ms** slot is not a viable timing-only substitute: Fly A's bit for the upcoming cue is derived from its learned controller's response to that cue's simulated MBON activity, which under the unchanged schedule is not available until the upcoming phase **240**. Reusing the previous round's bit would change the represented observation; obtaining the next bit in advance would require advancing Fly A's cue/neural simulation or replaying future recordings. Moreover, the next round's phase-50 count reset would erase prior-window spike counts from Fly B's decision-rate measurement. A causal pre-cue test therefore requires changes beyond one message-window shift, including source scheduling and/or trial/count handling.
- **Decision / controls / limitations.** The requested non-overlapping receiving window does **not** exist within the current causal RPS schedule while cue timing, source definition, and count/readout semantics stay fixed. Per the stated Stage-1 stop rule, no new probe seeds, decoder fitting, stimulation comparison, raw simulation artifact, or teacher-student training were run. The proposed >=10/12 bit and >=10/12 cue-recognition gates were not evaluated; the old failed receiver result is not replaced. A later protocol with an explicit pre-cue teacher lead and student prelude would be a different prospectively specified experiment, not a timing-only rescue of this one. No code or prior results were changed. Reproduce the timing audit with `rg -n "ROUND_MS|T_COUNT|T_DECIDE|T_LEARN|startRound|resetCounts|playRound|previousOpponent" js/rps.js` and `rg -n "elig =|afterDopamine" js/agents.js js/rps.js`; archived overlapping-window measurements remain in `data/rps_hybrid_receiver.json`.

### Prospective teacher-first 680 ms RPS receiving check (locked before implementation and simulation, )

- **Hypothesis / scope.** Moving the unchanged 40 ms one-bit KC stimulation before Fly B's ordinary 240 ms cue may leave a detectable trace in decision-time MBON rates without the severe cue interference measured under overlapping stimulation. This is one frozen receiving diagnostic, with no Fly B training, learning-speed claim, or retuning. The previous source bit **failed** its original action-leak gate; this revised hybrid question explicitly permits action-related advice, without changing that result.
- **Fixed states and streams.** Reconstruct trained contextual-bandit Fly A seed **711** and Fly B seed **712** from `data/rps_contextual_bandit.json`, using their saved final controller weights, all-one KC-to-MBON multipliers, and separate real-wiring networks. This deterministic first-two-archived-seeds choice is independent of receiver outcomes. All neural/controller weights and Fly B learning remain frozen. Each isolated observed-cue probe resets both neural states and uses one of three predetermined previous-opponent states **0,1,2**; the `previous_opponent` opponent advances only after Fly B decides. Matched no-message, forced-bit-0, and forced-bit-1 probes use the same state and network streams. For state `s` and repeat `r=0..17`, key `2026110400 + 1000*s + 10*r`; Fly A uses `rng(key+1)`, Fly B `rng(key+2)`. At 280 ms Fly B's random generator is restarted as `rng(key+3)` in every arm for matched cue-period draws, without resetting any neural state. Repeats **0-5** calibrate only the bit decoder; repeats **6-17** are untouched cue-recognition tests, with **6-11** the predefined 12-bit discrimination test (six probes per bit). Forced bits are crossed with every cue, independently of the actual teacher bit. One separate first-round validity probe has no previous move, sensory cue, or teacher message and is excluded from the gates. No state selection or stream replacement will occur.
